It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"109:1","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":9,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":15,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"109:1","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"109:1","surface_ref":"109:1","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"109:1","target_tokens":[["De",["109:1:1"]],["ki",["109:1:1"]],["Ey",["109:1:2"]],["inkâr",["109:1:3"]],["edenler",["109:1:3"]]],"text":"De ki: Ey inkâr edenler!"},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":9,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":6,"id":"s109-p01-001-006","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["109:1","109:2","109:3","109:4","109:5","109:6"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0055b523e8424567393d","text":"2. **The vocative diagnoses a service relation, not an undefined population.** Fixed **ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ** activates `ك ف ر B003`, covering or rejecting truth. The basmalah has named a worshipped one (`ء ل ه B001`), and 109:2–5 repeatedly contrasts objects of **عبد** (`ع ب د B003`). Those supplied relations let the covering branch operate specifically at the junction of recognized object and submissive service. The inferred arrow is that the worship mismatch embodies the named covering or rejection. The vocative therefore reads as a functional diagnosis of allegiance rather than a contextless social label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_09e52993816380e9b791","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:1:3\",\"root_001307\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Doğrudan hitap, yapılan nitelemeyi sınırsız bir mahkûmiyete çevirmek yerine muhatabı konuşmanın içinde tutar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0fd57ef27ad7244747db","text":"3. **The ayah refuses negotiated exchange.** The fixed root ق و ل also has B009, \"mutual verbal negotiation,\" while ك ف ر has B005, \"disavowal.\" The activator is the lack of any imperative or request after 109:1; instead, the surah proceeds through four negated worship clauses and then allotment in 109:6. Mechanism: the command to speak occupies the place where bargaining might have occurred, but the speech itself disavows exchange. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives a negotiation branch and disavowal branch; I infer that repeated negation blocks the negotiation pathway. Reading change: the address becomes a controlled non-negotiation, not an invitation to doctrinal compromise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_22b14c4964a868f81e85","text":"1. **The address exposes a covering before it judges a group.** The fixed ayah supplies ك ف ر B001, \"covering and concealment,\" and B003, \"covering or rejecting truth.\" The later activators are the repeated objects of worship in 109:2-5 and the final دِين split in 109:6. Mechanism: the addressees are not merely named by a social label; the address makes visible the covering relation that the later worship clauses will unfold. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives the covering/rejecting branches and later incompatible worship/dīn; I supply the arrow from hidden covering to public address. Reading change: \"ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ\" reads as diagnostic exposure of a concealed/rejected truth, not only as a fixed category.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B009","root_001307/B003","root_001307/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_402439267e951193025d","text":"The biggest later surprise was that 109:2-5 make \"ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ\" less static, not more. Because the following lines repeatedly specify non-overlapping worship-relations, ك ف ر B003 becomes a diagnosis unfolded by syntax rather than a bare label. 109:6 then restrains ك ف ر B006 ascription: the address names the addressees, but the final dīn allotment leaves each side with its own account rather than continuing accusation. The four negations also confirmed the non-negotiation reading from ق و ل B009; after قُلْ there is no bargaining sequence, only boundary speech.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4588d67b09cd2d0df33a","text":"4. **The speech stages a settlement by disavowal.** Fixed `ك ف ر B005` contributes disavowal, while the dormant negotiation image `ق و ل B009` is activated by the alternating “I/you” clauses and the balanced allocation **لَكُمْ … وَلِيَ …** in 109:6. Disavowal supplies relational separation; the reciprocal syntax supplies the shape of terms exchanged. The abductive move is that these mirrored propositions close negotiation rather than invite compromise. This is exploratory because **قُلْ**, not a reciprocal verb form, bears the saying root; nevertheless the full construction changes the line into the opening of a formal disentanglement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B013","root_001307/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_69c906b300fe96b53799","text":"The later **عبد** sequence in 109:2–5 corrected an initially free-standing reading of **ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ**: `ك ف ر B003` became a diagnosis enacted through incompatible service-object relations. More surprisingly, 109:6’s paired **دِين** activated `ق و ل B013` in **قُلْ**, so the utterance carries held doctrine, and its balanced allocations activated the latent `ق و ل B009`/`ك ف ر B005` mechanism of negotiated-form speech ending in disavowal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B009","root_001307/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6dee7dc2bd5e5a57faf8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:1:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B009\"]],[\"109:1:3\",\"root_001307\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karşılıklı biçimde düzenlenen cümleler, uzlaşma pazarlığı açmak yerine ayrılığı kayda bağlayan bir bildiri niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001","root_001272/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7793d17b66dabd32749f","text":"2. **The command \"say\" turns the boundary into public record.** The fixed ayah supplies ق و ل B001, \"uttered speech,\" and B007, \"circulating talk or public report.\" The activator is the exact repetition of denial in 109:2-5, which gives the utterance a formulaic, recitable profile. Mechanism: a spoken declaration becomes something that can circulate as a public report of non-participation. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives utterance and repeated clauses; I infer public-record force from their formulaic shape. Reading change: قُلْ is not a neutral instruction to pronounce words; it launches a publicly repeatable boundary statement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_78ef937d63dcb7a0e6ad","text":"1. **The saying is a public doctrinal act.** Fixed **قُلْ** directly activates `ق و ل B001` (uttered speech), while `ق و ل B013` (belief or held doctrine) is awakened by the ensuing worship claims and the concluding **دِين** (`د ي ن B001`, a way of obedience and submission). The imperative and the six-ayah scope of its content make articulation itself the assigned action: an allegiance is to be voiced before the addressed plural. I infer that commanded public articulation makes the held position socially accountable. Thus **قُلْ** is more than neutral transmission; it externalizes a doctrine of service.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8095ca0f638371aed57f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:1:3\",\"root_001307\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Önceki merhamet ifadesi, inkârı kendilerine sunulan iyiliği tanımama boyutuyla da yükler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001","root_001272/B007","root_001272/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cc69b9f4ba6c22a6b5dd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:1:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\",\"B007\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"“De” buyruğu, izleyen sınırı herkes önünde söylenebilir ve aktarılabilir bir inanç beyanına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_df9871ae8e88f47dc07d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"109:1:3\",\"root_001307\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hitap, muhatapları donmuş bir toplumsal etiketle sınıflamak yerine, sonraki ibadet ayrılıklarının açığa çıkardığı hakikati örtme ilişkisini teşhis eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e9aab9e797eb403bc2c7","text":"4. **Ascription is present but bounded by direct address.** ك ف ر B006, \"ascription of unbelief,\" is activated by the vocative form يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ. The later activator is 109:6, where each side is assigned its own دِين rather than the speaker absorbing the other side's account. Mechanism: the ayah performs naming/ascription, yet the direct address keeps the named party present as an interlocutor rather than turning them into an absent object. The abductive move is mine: the packet gives ascription and final allocation; I infer a bounded naming act rather than unlimited condemnation. Reading change: the vocative becomes a precise speech-act of designation inside a larger separation protocol.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f0b9a256a6f2f67d424a","text":"3. **The address can include covered beneficence.** Fixed `ك ف ر B004` supplies failure to acknowledge a favor; the immediately prior doubled **رحم** supplies beneficence (`ر ح م B001`). The adjacency assigns mercy the role of offered good and **كفر** the role of concealing its status as good. I infer, rather than receive from the packet, that the addressees’ designation includes this response to mercy. This expands the address from denial of a proposition to nonacknowledgment of beneficent relation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The repeated ع ب د context moves ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ from a proposition-centered label into a distinction enacted through incompatible allegiances.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_649779d10db5e6b01e96","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"Because the classifier is himself commanded, later ownership and sovereignty images strengthen a reading of delegated rather than self-arrogated authority.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/3","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_796dd48eb8a676441aeb","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The focus inventories produced unusually live friction between speech, classification, and covering: saying could disclose, negotiate, define, or risk judgment, while the addressee noun could mark rejection, disavowal, material concealment, and hierarchy.
